- **bdi-models-and-systems-reducing-the-gap**: Design executable BDI reasoning using scoped explicit negation, Event Calculus, abductive feasibility checks, and preference-governed revision. The cited Móra et al. paper is a cognitive-model proposal, assumes initially consistent beliefs, and leaves belief update out of scope. Use for runtime semantics and conflicting candidate desires. NOT for purely axiomatic modal logic, black-box planners, or classical logic without operational semantics. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/tree/HEAD/docs/research/agent-skills-expansion-20260924/drafts-pending-review/bdi-models-and-systems-reducing-the-gap) · ★3)

NOT for purely axiomatic modal logic, black-box planners, or classical logic without operational semantics. ([curiositech/port-daddy](https://github.com/curiositech/port-daddy/tree/HEAD/docs/research/agent-skills-expansion-20260924/drafts-pending-review/bdi-models-and-systems-reducing-the-gap-paper) · ★3)
